1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any dp module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 348. The projective resolution has length at most 976 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to dp theory, we decompose the problem into 348 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 976. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the dp structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 672. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 403 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by dp generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 672, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 403. QED.
